Use + New trip in the left rail to create one here."*) · tab strip reads Trip first · trip created with 10 day cards · trip edited and both the rail row and the selected card refreshed · `+ Stop` correctly disabled at zero regions · region created with all six fields and the **soft out-of-range date warning** firing and clearing · region edited including a summary cleared to empty, with the `clear_summary` flag path confirmed by reopening the drawer · stop created, edited, reparented and moved across regions · stop delete review in-panel, with the **children-promoted** copy verified live (*"they move up to become top-level stops in the same region"*) · both region-delete stages · disposable trip removed through the Phase 3A force-delete gate · legacy fallback toggled out and back · **zero console errors**. **The mount/socket/listener census was made functional, not visual.** Counting `.tdl-root` only proves the DOM; a leaked subscription is invisible there. So the census wraps `WebSocket` and `BroadcastChannel` and drives a full legacy round trip with the Lori pane open: **1 socket created / 1 closed / 1 live**, **channels 6 created / 5 closed / 1 live**, one mount throughout. Then a duplicate-listener probe posted a single trip-saved message on the named channel and counted the bundle reload — **all eight endpoints fetched exactly once**, which is the assertion that would read 2 if the toggle had leaked a second mount. The first attempt at that probe returned zero fetches and proved nothing, because the handler correctly ignores a trip id that is not the open trip; a probe that cannot fail is decoration, so it was rerun against a real selected trip. **Files changed:** `ui/js/travel-doc-lab.js`, `ui/css/travel-doc-lab.css`, `tests/test_travel_doc_lab.py`, `tests/test_travel_doc_evidence_ui.py`, `docs/wo/WO-TRAVEL-DOC-UNIFY-01_Spec.md`, `MASTER_WORK_ORDER_CHECKLIST.md`, `CLAUDE.md`. **Files added:** none. **Files removed:** five `_p3b_patch_*.py` scratch files (container-only; they never reached the repo). **Flag state: unchanged.** **Active baseline unchanged:** `r5h-followup-guard-v1` 78/114, v3=49/72, v2=43/72, mnw=2 — no extractor-lane code touched. **Open items:** Phase 3C (photo/source upload + cluster), 3D (route board / reorder / insert ergonomics), then Phase 4 (delete the legacy fallback and the old Documenter), 5 (test consolidation), 6 (full live smoke). Backlog carried, explicitly not gates: the bio-suggestion-blocks-every-delete question stays a **backend/product** call and is still outside the frontend wall; selection still does not survive a tab round-trip (fix by passing saved state into `lvTravelDocMount()`, not by keeping a hidden mount alive). The CSS added this phase is structural only — every colour reuses an existing `--tdl-*` variable, because the theme item is **retired at Chris's instruction**, not deferred. Also still open from 2026-07-23: live smoke of `WO-EVIDENCE-LIFECYCLE-TRIP-FORCE-01`. A phase is a scope wall.

- 2026-07-25 (**WO-TRAVEL-DOC-UNIFY-01 Phase 3A LANDED** — the trip force-delete impact-review gate now exists in the unified Travel Doc workspace): Front-end only; **no backend, no API, no schema, no flag change**. Chris's wall: *"Start with the force-delete impact review gate, because it is the safety-critical production-only control."* No route board, no upload, no cluster, no region/stop CRUD, no theme pass; `travel-documenter.js` untouched and the legacy fallback still reachable. **The shape:** the selected-trip card in the sidebar grows a `tdl-btn-danger` “Delete trip” control; it attempts a plain `DELETE /api/trips/{id}` first; a **409** puts the structured impact payload into `st.deleteReview` and `renderAll()` appends an in-panel review; force delete then re-sends with `{force:true, confirm_trip_id, reason}`. **Prerequisite fix:** the lab's single `api()` choke point discarded the response body on error, so the gate could not have read an impact payload at all — the error arm now attaches `err.status` and `err.body`, and the gate reads `e.body.detail`, not `e.body`, because FastAPI nests structured payloads under `detail` (the pinning assertion lands in the **same commit** as the port, exactly as the spec demanded). **Zero native dialogs:** all eight `confirm`/`prompt` textual matches in the file are inside comments; the arming control is a text input compared against the exact trip title *or* the trip id, and the drawer holds live DOM references in closures so typing never repaints — verified live across 27 real keystrokes with focus retained. Handlers are assigned by property (`input.oninput = fn`), never `addEventListener`, so reopening the review for a different trip cannot stack a stale closure. **The port is deliberately not verbatim, because production's grid is wrong.** `travel-documenter.js` renders **nine** impact lanes and never mentions `bio_suggestions`, but `trip_repository._TRIP_DEPENDENT_TABLES` has **ten**, and `trip_timeline_bridge.sync_trip_to_life_record` writes one `travel.trip` bio suggestion on **every trip create** — so **every trip is force-delete-only from birth**, and in production the operator sees an all-zero impact grid next to a refused delete. Proved live, not theorised: a disposable trip with no dates, no regions and no notes returned 409 with every lane zero except `1 Bio suggestions`. The unified port renders all ten. Whether a self-generated bio suggestion should block an unforced delete is a backend/product call and is **outside this phase's frontend-only wall** — backlog, not scope creep. **Three blanket “the lab never DELETEs” test guards were narrowed, not deleted.** The file-wide form stopped being true the moment the phase ported one sanctioned destructive control, but dropping the tests would have retired the properties they were actually protecting, so they now assert what matters: every DELETE targets `/api/trips/` plus a trip id and none targets an evidence lane (evidence is hide-only — PATCH, never DELETE), and the reconcile drawer and its loader contain no DELETE at all (reconcile *reviews* missing and outside-date days, it never silently cleans them up). **Live smoke, all ten of Chris's steps green:** 409 opens the drawer with all ten counts · a case-mismatched title leaves the button disabled and fires no request · the exact trip id arms · the exact title arms · force delete returns 200 · the trip disappears from the server and the rail · nothing is auto-selected afterwards (“Select a trip from the left rail.”) · exactly one mount at every point · legacy fallback round-trips cleanly · zero console errors. **205 tests green** (was 192). The code commit also carries a one-line Phase 2 test repair — `MountContractTest::test_opts_are_preferred_over_the_querystring` still asserted the pre-Phase-2 `opts.apiBase || qsParams.get` form, which left main red; it rides along because it lives in the same file and hunk staging is banned here. **Files changed:** `ui/js/travel-doc-lab.js`, `ui/css/travel-doc-lab.css`, `tests/test_travel_doc_lab.py`, `tests/test_travel_doc_evidence_ui.py`, `docs/wo/WO-TRAVEL-DOC-UNIFY-01_Spec.md`, `MASTER_WORK_ORDER_CHECKLIST.md`, `CLAUDE.md`. **Files added:** none. **Flag state: unchanged.** **Active baseline unchanged:** `r5h-followup-guard-v1` 78/114, v3=49/72, v2=43/72, mnw=2 — no extractor-lane code touched. **Open items:** Phase 3B (trip create/edit + region/stop CRUD — and region/stop delete must land as in-panel review, never `window.confirm()`), 3C (photo/source upload + cluster), 3D (route board / reorder / insert-at-position), then Phases 4-6. Backlog carried, explicitly not gates: the no-trips empty state still says “Create one in the production Travel Doc tab” (seen live); selection does not survive a tab round-trip (fix by passing saved state into `lvTravelDocMount()`, not by keeping a hidden mount alive); and the bio-suggestion-blocks-every-delete question above. **Retired at Chris's instruction:** the light-on-cream theme-mismatch item — *"i like how the styles look on the travel doc so leav it as it is."* A phase is a scope wall.

- 2026-07-25 (**WO-TRAVEL-DOC-UNIFY-01 Phase 2 LANDED** — the unified Travel Doc workspace now mounts inside the shell's Travel Doc tab and is the default operator surface): Front-end only; **no backend, no API, no schema, no flag change**. No route-editing port, no upload/cluster port, no delete-gate port, `travel-documenter.js` still present — all Phase 3+ by Chris's stated non-goals. **The shape:** the tab holds `#lvTravelDocUnifiedHost` and `#lvTravelDocHost` plus a two-button surface switch; `_lvTravelDocSurface()` reads `localStorage["lvTravelDocSurface"]` and defaults anything that is not the literal `"legacy"` to `"unified"`, so a corrupt or absent value lands on the default path rather than the fallback. `lvShellShowTab` destroys the surface it is not showing before mounting the one it is; leaving the tab tears down both. **Exactly one live mount is a correctness rule, not tidiness** — each surface owns a `hornelore-trip-updates` BroadcastChannel subscription, a `document`-level keydown listener and a Lori `/api/chat/ws` socket, so two mounts double all three and both keydown handlers answer Escape. **Two pre-existing leaks closed:** the shell already called `lvTravelDocumenterMount()` and threw the handle away, leaking that surface's keydown listener and modal-Lori socket on every narrator switch; and `travel-documenter.js` opened its trip-update channel at mount and never closed it — tolerable at one mount per narrator, not once Phase 2 destroys and remounts on every tab exit, narrator switch and surface toggle. Touching the Documenter is inside the wall: the non-goal is *do not remove it yet*, not *do not touch it*. **CSS scoping — the risk Chris named going in:** the 16 `--tdl-*` custom properties moved from `:root` to `.tdl-root` and the bare element resets were rescoped beneath it. Custom-property inheritance is DOM-based rather than layout-based, so the module's three `position: fixed` overlays (`.tdl-drawer-scrim`, `.tdl-drawer`, `.tdl-lightbox`) still resolve through the host and were deliberately left alone. `lvTravelDocMount()` owns `.tdl-root`/`.tdl-root-embedded` and `destroy()` removes them, so the stylesheet is inert in the shell until something mounts. `tdl-` prefix unchanged. **Lab framing gone from the operator path:** the `WO-TRAVEL-DOC-LAB-LAUNCH-BUTTON-01` block — the unstyled "🧪 Open Travel Doc UI Lab" button that opened a second browser tab — is deleted; the module gates its badge and eval checklist on `opts.embedded`, the picker reads "Travel Doc", and `?person_id=`/`?api=` are quarantined to the standalone page so identity comes from `opts`. `ui/travel-doc-lab.html` untouched and still works. **Verification:** `node --check` clean on all three JS files and both inline blocks of `hornelore1.0.html`; **192 tests green** (the five lab-reading suites plus the new `tests/test_travel_doc_shell_mount.py`, 38 tests). Static tests pin shape and cannot watch a census, so behaviour is proved by a second headless script, `scripts/ui/run_travel_doc_shell_mount_liveness.js`, which loads the **real** `hornelore1.0.html`, parks every `fetch`, and drives `lvShellShowTab`/`lvTravelDocSetSurface` as an operator would — 9 rows, a destroyed-repaint scenario, a branding scan, **22 checks PASS**, zero unhandled rejections, zero page errors. **It caught three defects the static tests could not.** (1) `function _lvTravelDocSurface()` cached on `window._lvTravelDocSurface`; `app.js` is a classic script with no IIFE, so that identifier IS the function — the first call overwrote itself with the string `"unified"` and the second threw *"_lvTravelDocSurface is not a function"*. Cache moved to `window._lvTravelDocActiveSurface`, and a new test now fails on any `window.foo = …` in `app.js` shadowing a top-level `function foo()`. (2) The socket census tested the URL for `/travel_doc/`, but both modules dial `apiBase + "/api/chat/ws"` — the regex matched nothing and every `ws` assertion was decoration; attribution is now by call stack, with a non-vacuity guard that opens the Lori pane and requires exactly one socket. (3) The destroyed-repaint observer was installed before the navigation and counted `destroy()`'s own cleanup as a repaint; the window narrowed, with a separate `tornDown` assertion keeping the cleanup under test. **Negative controls actually run, not claimed:** deleting the tab-exit `lvTravelDocTeardownAll()` call turns 5 checks red; deleting **both** toggle guards turns 5 red with the census at 2/2 and both hosts painted — the exact defect Chris named as the top risk; deleting **either** toggle guard alone still passes, so the two are **mutually redundant** and a green run must not be read as proof that both are load-bearing; reverting the socket attribution turns the non-vacuity guard red. **Known behaviour change:** leaving the Travel Doc tab destroys the mount, so trip/day selection does not survive a tab round-trip — a deliberate reading of the acceptance line *"Switching away/remounting calls destroy()"*, since a hidden panel is `display:none` and not unloaded, and the alternative leaves a socket and a `document` keydown listener live under whatever tab the operator is actually looking at. If that cost is not worth paying, the fix is to persist selection in `opts`, not to stop destroying. **Files changed:** `ui/js/app.js`, `ui/hornelore1.0.html`, `ui/js/travel-doc-lab.js`, `ui/js/travel-documenter.js`, `ui/css/travel-doc-lab.css`, `ui/css/lori80.css`, `docs/wo/WO-TRAVEL-DOC-UNIFY-01_Spec.md`, `MASTER_WORK_ORDER_CHECKLIST.md`, `CLAUDE.md`. **Files added:** `tests/test_travel_doc_shell_mount.py`, `scripts/ui/run_travel_doc_shell_mount_liveness.js`. **Flag state: unchanged.** **Active baseline unchanged:** `r5h-followup-guard-v1` 78/114, v3=49/72, v2=43/72, mnw=2 — no extractor-lane code touched. **Open items:** Phase 3 (port the Documenter's features in — delete gate first, then upload, then the route board), then Phases 4-6. The surface switch and `travel-documenter.js` are both deleted in Phase 4. A phase is a scope wall.

- 2026-07-24 (**WO-TRAVEL-DOC-UNIFY-01 Phase 1.1 LANDED** — the mount can no longer be repainted after `destroy()`): Front-end only; **no backend, no API, no schema, no flag change, no shell change**. Chris's review of Phase 1 accepted the mountable-module refactor but found the hole: *"destroy() closes the BroadcastChannel, resets Lori, and clears the host — good. But I do not see a destroyed/mounted-alive guard that prevents pending async callbacks from rendering after destroy()."* Correct. Phase 1 could not surface it because the standalone harness never unmounts; Phase 2 mounts and unmounts on every tab switch, which is exactly when an in-flight `loadTrips` / bundle load / evidence reload / Lori drawer refresh resolves into a host the caller already cleared. Chris held Phase 2 until this landed. **Why six guards and not fifty-four.** The file has 54 `.then(` call sites; guarding each is unreviewable and rots on the next feature. Surveying instead found the file has exactly one of each guardable thing — one `fetch(` (inside `api()`), one repaint entry point (`renderAll()`, which all 24 `render*` functions route through), one BroadcastChannel handler, one WebSocket `onmessage`, one timer (the Lori send-retry ladder), one `document`-level listener (`keydown`) — so six guards give total coverage. The tests pin every one of those counts, so a seventh async path invalidates the strategy loudly instead of leaking silently. **`abandoned()`** is `new Promise(function () {})`, a promise that never settles; `api()` returns it when the mount is dead so neither `.then()` nor `.catch()` runs at any of the 54 sites. Rejecting would fire every `.catch(e => { st.error = e.message; renderAll(); })` in the file — itself a write to dead state; resolving would fire every success path. Never settling is the only silent option. **Socket-identity pinning** (`var sock = this.ws` in `connect()`, `if (destroyed || self.ws !== sock) return;` in `onmessage`) covers a case `destroyed` alone does not: `loriPane.reset()` runs on every **trip switch**, not just teardown, nulling `this.ws` while the old socket may still deliver a queued frame — without the pin a token from Trip A's stream appends into Trip B's transcript. That is a live-mount bug the liveness work exposed, not a teardown one. **The leak the review did not name:** `document.addEventListener("keydown", ...)` is the only listener bound outside the host, so clearing the host never removed it — two mounts meant two live listeners on `document`, and after `destroy()` an arrow key still drove `lightboxStep()` -> `renderAll()`. Named `onDocKeydown` and unbound in `destroy()`. `destroy()` raises the flag **first**, before any teardown step, because each step can run script that re-enters the module; it stays idempotent and every step stays individually guarded. **Verification:** `node --check` clean, **154 tests green** across the five lab-reading suites (was 142; `test_travel_doc_lab` gained 12). The static pins check the *shape* of the guards and cannot watch a stale callback land on a dead host, so that is proved in a real browser by the new `scripts/ui/run_travel_doc_mount_liveness.js` (no backend, no manual server, no arguments, exits 0/1). Its method is the point: `window.fetch` is replaced with one that **parks** every request, and with fetch parked `boot()` paints nothing at all because `renderAll()` lives inside the `.then()` — so "host is empty" is the identical starting state for every row and the only variable is whether the mount was destroyed before the release. `control_live` (not destroyed, released 200 OK) -> 3 mutations / 1 child; `destroyed_then` (200 OK), `destroyed_notok` (500 + error body) and `destroyed_reject` (network rejection) -> 0 mutations / 0 children each. `control_live` is load-bearing: without it, three "nothing happened" results prove nothing, since a harness that never delivers a callback also produces three empty hosts. Censuses: `hornelore-trip-updates` subscriptions 2 -> 1 -> 0 across two mounts and two destroys, `document` keydown registrations 2 -> 1 -> 0. Both count bind/unbind rather than observing effects on purpose — the keydown handler early-returns unless a lightbox is open, so an "assert no repaint on keypress" test would pass with the listener still bound, vacuously. Zero unhandled rejections, zero page errors. **Negative controls run by hand, both confirmed red:** (1) `destroy()` set to `destroyed = false` -> all three destroyed rows flipped to 3 mutations / 1 child, reproducing Chris's reported bug exactly; (2) `destroy()`'s `removeEventListener` line deleted -> keydown census went 2 -> 2 -> 2. A green suite that cannot go red is decoration; re-run both by hand if the guards change. **Not exercised:** the shell mount path, still — `hornelore1.0.html` untouched, that is Phase 2 by design. **Files changed:** `ui/js/travel-doc-lab.js` (+104/-3), `tests/test_travel_doc_lab.py` (+216), `docs/wo/WO-TRAVEL-DOC-UNIFY-01_Spec.md`, `MASTER_WORK_ORDER_CHECKLIST.md`, `CLAUDE.md`. **Files added:** `scripts/ui/run_travel_doc_mount_liveness.js`. **Flag state: unchanged.** **Active baseline unchanged:** `r5h-followup-guard-v1` 78/114, v3=49/72, v2=43/72, mnw=2 — no extractor-lane code touched. **Open items:** Phase 2 (mount into the Travel Doc tab behind a temporary fallback toggle, old Documenter still reachable) is now unblocked, and carries the launcher-CSS discoverability fix — `lvTravelDocLabBtn` / `lv-td-lab-launch` / `lv-td-lab-hint` still have zero CSS anywhere in `ui/`. Phases 3-6 after it. A phase is a scope wall.

- 2026-07-24 (**WO-TRAVEL-DOC-UNIFY-01 Phase 1 LANDED** — the Travel Doc Lab is now a mountable module): Front-end only; **no backend, no API, no schema, no flag change**. Origin (Chris): *"I dont want a cosmetic fix, I want one merged best of what we have on the two and do away with the lab and have it just be the travel docs."* The repo carries two divergent Travel Doc implementations — production `ui/js/travel-documenter.js` (2737 lines, `td-` prefix, one-shot `template()` → `innerHTML` → imperative `data-td` wiring) and `ui/js/travel-doc-lab.js` (3414 lines pre-phase, `tdl-` prefix, state object + `renderAll()` re-render loop + preserved scroll + `dayFormDirtyBlocks()` dirty-guard). **Direction decided: the Lab absorbs the Documenter**, because either direction rewrites the losing file at roughly equal cost and only this one keeps the newer render architecture — and because the Lab was built with a socket for exactly this (`renderCurrent()` at line 597 is a placeholder reading *"Baseline lives in production"*: the Lab's authors carved out a space the size and shape of the Documenter and declined to fill it). **Phase 1 is behaviour-neutral by construction:** the whole module body becomes the body of `window.lvTravelDocMount(hostEl, opts) -> {destroy()}`; every former module-level binding (`st`, `root`, `railCollapsed`, `insOpen`, `_tdlUpdateChannel`, `photoEvidence`, `lightbox`, `loriPane`) is scoped PER MOUNT so two mounts cannot collide; `opts.person_id` / `opts.apiBase` / `opts.person_label` take precedence with the querystring kept as the standalone page's fallback; `root = hostEl || document.getElementById("tdlRoot")`; `boot()` moves inside the mount and the bare page-scope call is gone. **The body is deliberately NOT re-indented** — re-indenting 3,400 lines would bury a 4-edit diff in a whitespace change and make review impossible; the mount boundary is banner-commented at head and foot instead. **Amendment A1 (`destroy()`) is load-bearing, not cosmetic:** `"hornelore-trip-updates"` is a NAMED BroadcastChannel, so two live mounts mean two subscriptions on the same name and one cross-tab trip save fires two refreshes (double re-render in the shell, flake in tests). `destroy()` closes the channel, drops the Lori socket via `loriPane.reset()`, clears the host, guards every step individually, and is idempotent — a teardown that throws strands a caller mid-swap. **One pin moved outward, flagged deliberately:** `test_page_loads_only_lab_assets` relaxed from "exactly one `<script>` tag" to "exactly one `<script src=>`, and it is the lab's own" — the harness needs an inline mount call, and the property that test exists to protect is *no foreign assets*, not *no inline code*. **Verification:** `node --check` clean; **142 tests green** across the five suites that read the lab source (`test_travel_doc_lab`, `test_travel_doc_evidence_ui`, `test_travel_doc_livetest_fixes`, `test_trip_lane_fixpack_js`, `test_travel_documenter_panel`); mount lifecycle driven in a **headless browser** rather than asserted by inspection, with `BroadcastChannel` instrumented to count live subscriptions — page load 1 channel + root rendered; second mount into a fresh host 2 + renders independently; `destroy()` the second back to 1 with only that host cleared and the first still fully rendered; repeat `destroy()` a no-op. Phase 1's acceptance criterion met literally. **Not exercised: the shell mount path (`#lvTravelDocHost`) — that is Phase 2 by design;** `hornelore1.0.html` is untouched in this phase. **Discoverability defect recorded (fix in Phase 2):** the `WO-TRAVEL-DOC-LAB-LAUNCH-BUTTON-01` launcher block in `hornelore1.0.html` (~3600–3617) has **zero CSS anywhere in `ui/`** for `lvTravelDocLabBtn` / `lv-td-lab-launch` / `lv-td-lab-hint`, which is why it renders as plain text and Chris could not find it. **Amendments folded into the spec:** A1 unmount (landed); **A2 — the Lab has zero `FormData` and zero file inputs**, so photo/source upload is a capability to BUILD, not a control to port (its own comments admit the punt: line 864 "new uploads still come in via Photo Intake", line 1796 cluster happens "from the production Travel Doc"); A3 explicit retire list so the port has a closing condition (ping/Check API, apiBase+personId inputs, Load trips, output console + statusLine, the "Baseline lives in production" banner, the lab evaluation checklist, the EXPERIMENTAL chip — everything NOT on that list ports or blocks); A4 route editing is wider than the outline suggests (region area/start/end/base/summary + stop region-selector/type + insert-at-position `insertContext`/`insertHint`); **A5 — `travel-documenter.js` lines 2131 (region delete) and 2153 (stop delete) call `window.confirm()` and MUST NOT port as-is**, or they regress the exact destructive-action posture `WO-EVIDENCE-LIFECYCLE-TRIP-FORCE-01` established (the Lab is currently clean: its only two `window.prompt` matches, 2039 and 2203, are comments stating the doctrine); A6 Phase 6 scope trim (OCR / textless-fail-safe / public-lookup / blocked-localhost exercise the photo-evidence lane, not the merge — run them, do not let them gate unification sign-off). **Two conventions settled up front:** keep the `tdl-` class prefix (renaming to `td-` collides with the Documenter during Phase 2 coexistence and yields a 3000-line diff that changes nothing — rename the files, keep the prefix), and scope the Lab's `body`-level CSS onto `.tdl-root` before it enters the dark shell. **Files changed:** `ui/js/travel-doc-lab.js` (140,645 b / 3,472 lines), `ui/travel-doc-lab.html` (reduced to a thin harness that calls the mount; the handle is parked on `window` so teardown is drivable from the console), `tests/test_travel_doc_lab.py` (new `MountContractTest` pins the entry point, caller-supplied host, opts-over-querystring precedence, absence of a page-scope `boot()`, and the `destroy()` handle), `MASTER_WORK_ORDER_CHECKLIST.md`, `CLAUDE.md`. **Files added:** `docs/wo/WO-TRAVEL-DOC-UNIFY-01_Spec.md`. **Flag state: unchanged** — this WO introduces no `HORNELORE_*` flag and flips none. **Active baseline unchanged:** `r5h-followup-guard-v1` 78/114, v3=49/72, v2=43/72, mnw=2 (no extractor-lane code touched; expected byte-stable). **Open items:** Phases 2–6 — Ph2 coexist in the shell tab behind a toggle + the launcher CSS fix; Ph3 port the Documenter in, **delete gate FIRST** (newest and least-exercised code in either panel, and its `e.body.detail` error-envelope pin must move in the SAME commit as the port so it never lapses); Ph4 flip and delete `travel-documenter.js` / `travel-documenter.css` / `travel-doc-lab.html` / the launcher block, renaming survivors to `travel-doc.js` / `travel-doc.css`; Ph5 fold the two test files into `tests/test_travel_doc.py` — a rewrite, not a merge, because `test_travel_doc_lab.py` asserts the Lab does NOT reference the production module and that boundary inverts under this WO; Ph6 live smoke. **A phase is a scope wall — Phase 2 is a separate session.** Also still open from 2026-07-23: live smoke of `WO-EVIDENCE-LIFECYCLE-TRIP-FORCE-01`.

- 2026-07-14 (**INC-2026-07-09 CLOSED — response guards were dead in production for 5 days** + narrator-dignity fix batch + OCR evidence hardening): Live-testing session that started on OCR and ended on a critical outage. **(1) CRITICAL — every narrator-facing response guard was silently disabled from 2026-07-09 22:16 until the 2026-07-14 restart.** `_META_REASONING_RX` in `lori_response_guards.py` carried a SECOND inline `(?i)` before its alternation — a DeprecationWarning on py3.10, a hard `re.error` on py3.11+ ("global flags not at the start of the expression at position 99"). The server runs 3.12, so the module died AT IMPORT, and `chat_ws` imported it inside the per-turn `try/except` whose job is "never break a turn on guard failure" — which caught the ImportError, logged one WARNING, and served every reply UNGUARDED. `narrator_echo`, `meta_response_leak`, `dangling_determiner`, `language_drift`, the "I can see" block: all off, every turn. **The unit tests passed the whole time because the dev sandbox is py3.10.** Surfaced live when Lori parroted the narrator's sentence back in the FIRST PERSON ("My father built the back porch himself. That's a specific memory.") — the echo guard was working perfectly and was never called. Forensics: last guard fire 2026-07-09 16:13; `_META_REASONING_RX` added that same evening in the Build 1.5 leak-hardening batch; first crash 22:16. The regex written to catch Lori's meta-leaks killed every guard, including the meta-leak guard it belonged to. Fix: flag moved into `re.compile(..., re.IGNORECASE)`; **guards imported at MODULE SCOPE in chat_ws so a broken guards module fails the BOOT** (a stack that refuses to start beats one that quietly talks to an 86-year-old with no protections); new build gate `tests/test_regex_inline_flags_py311.py` enforces py3.11+ regex strictness regardless of interpreter, sweeping all of `server/code/api` in a CLEAN SUBPROCESS (the gate must not itself mutate `sys.modules` — the class that already poisons this suite via 27 modules stubbing `pydantic`), with a group-form detector (`(?im)`/`(?is)`/`(?msx)`, allowing scoped `(?i:...)`). Verified by injection: original bug RED, combined `(?im)` RED, fix GREEN. Zero other offenders. Post-restart: 23 `wrapper raised` before, **0** after. Write-up: `docs/incidents/INC-2026-07-09_RESPONSE-GUARDS-DISABLED.md`. **Standing lesson: a defensive `except` around an import is a silencer, not a safety net — runtime failure degrades, structural failure must fail loud, and the two must not share a handler.** **(2) BUG-SAFETY-DIRECTIVE-CONCATENATED-INTO-NARRATOR-TURN-01** — the UI did `parsed.message += _wsContextBlock`, appending its posture directive INTO the narrator's own message. The turn archived for a narrator in crisis was *"I don't want to be here anymore. There's no point to any of it. [SAFETY MODE: ACTIVE … Do NOT say 'I cannot continue' … Do NOT reset to interview mode.]"* — permanently contaminating their most vulnerable sentence in the memoir source, feeding directive text to the extractor and story-trigger, and getting mined by the follow-up bank as an ANCHOR so Lori recited it back: *"From what you just shared, I heard about Do NOT."* Safety mode is sticky, so the NEXT turn was polluted too. Not only safety — `companion`, `companion_override`, `memory_exercise_fact_seed` all rode the same path. Directive now travels BESIDE the message (`params.ui_context_block`) into the SYSTEM prompt via `compose_system_prompt(ui_system=)`, sanitized + capped at 1200 chars. Verified live: stored turns clean, **988 still routes**, no echo. **(3) BUG-MODAL-TURNS-ARCHIVED-AS-LIFE-STORY-01** — the archive write was gated on `person_id` and never on surface, so Travel Doc MODAL turns landed in the narrator's life-story archive as things the NARRATOR said; `peek_at_memoir`/`compose_memory_echo` read those to build "what you've shared so far", so operator workspace chatter became narrator memory. Both archive writes now surface-gated (assistant-side gate recomputed locally, not inherited from a binding ~3k lines up where an early return would NameError archiving for EVERY narrator). **(4) BUG-NARRATOR-LABEL-COLLISION-01** — `_horneloreNormalizeVisibleName()` canonicalizes ANY "christopher" to "Christopher Todd Horne", so two distinct people (`e7fdb578` + `a4b2f07a`) rendered with the IDENTICAL picker label. In a system whose job is attributing a life to the right person, that is how a memory lands in the wrong history. First fix used a birth-year suffix — **the live data defeated it: both Christophers share DOB 1962**. Now verifies the year actually distinguishes and falls back to the person_id. **(5) BUG-LORI-ERA-LABEL-IN-NARRATOR-PROSE-01** — pass-2A handed Lori the template `"...during your {era_label}?"`, so she said the title-cased Life Map heading out loud to an 86-year-old ("during those Coming of Age years"). DATA fix per the locked 2026-05-02 Patch B principle (prompt-heavy rules make Lori worse): new `_era_spoken_phrase()` hands her the speakable form from `lv_eras`, degrading to "that time in your life" rather than ever falling back to the label. **(6) OCR evidence hardening** — the confidence gate stopped NEW hallucinations but never removed OLD ones, and Lori kept reading a pre-gate row aloud for a photo of FOOD ("The OCR draft appears to read '# : 9 #4 - s 4 | | di i s k EJ…'", 1537 chars). `photo_context_supersede_drafts()` now retires prior unapproved drafts on success AND on rejection (a rejection is a FINDING: there is no text here); rows marked rejected, never DELETEd; APPROVED rows never touched. Live DB swept: 35 stale rows retired, 0 leaks. MEASURED across all 13 trip photos and recorded in `ocr_min_confidence()`: **the confidence distributions OVERLAP** (worst hallucination 63.4 outscores best real sign 50.1) — no floor separates them, so the correct 12/13 split needs confidence AND shape together; the ZUBR mug (real text, 45.5) sits below four of five textless photos and is **NOT recoverable by tuning**. Rejections now report the confidence they saw ("best pass: confidence 48, 64 words, floor 55") + an operator `?min_confidence=` override that cannot promote anything (row stays `draft`). Also verified live: public lookup reaches Lori with provenance wording, SSRF guard holds, no "I can see", no coordinates. **Corrections to my own reporting:** I flagged a "readiness gate stuck" bug and a "GREEN-while-loading badge" — **both were my measurement errors** (`window._llmReady` doesn't exist; the flag is module-scoped behind `isLlmReady()`, and my banner selector matched an inner child, not the parent carrying `.hidden`). No readiness bug exists. I also wrote the build gate wrong twice before it was right (cached `sys.modules`; then `find()` returning the FIRST flag at position 0 and missing the second at 99). **Still open:** extractor junk binding `residence.period="mostly"` (BINDING-01's lane); no live `fired=narrator_echo` line captured because the LLM has not parroted since the restart — the boot-time import guarantee is the stronger proof and absence-of-parrot is NOT evidence the guard fired.

- 2026-04-22 (midday): **WO-EX-SILENT-OUTPUT-01 Phase 1 instrumentation landed.** Five-counter cause-cascade wired into `extract.py` around the primary extraction loop (L6609–6927): `silent_llm_return`, `silent_parse_count`, `silent_post_validate_count`, `silent_fallback_count`, plus a terminal `[extract][silent-root]` log line classifying silent events into one of `llm_empty` / `parse_drop` / `validator_drop` / `fallback_miss` / `rules_filter_drop`. Raw-output dump (`raw_output[:300]`) gated behind default-off `HORNELORE_SILENT_DEBUG=1` flag. `probe_01` (`scripts/run_silent_output_probe.py`, 5-case diagnostic pack: case_005/045/065/077/081) + console confirmed instrumentation plumbed cleanly end-to-end. Characterization: `parse_drop` dominant (4 of 5 silent events) — LLM was returning text, parser was rejecting it. Review note: the `fallback_miss` branch in the cause cascade is logically subsumed by branches 1–3 (residual always falls to `rules_filter_drop`); not a correctness bug, but naming is aspirational — worth a TODO to tighten if the classifier ever sees cases that should distinguish "validator dropped everything AND fallback also produced nothing" from "rules filter subsequently dropped the surviving items." Zero extractor-behavior change from Phase 1 (pure instrumentation). **WO-EX-SILENT-OUTPUT-01 Phase 2 salvage patch landed** — one-line tolerance fix to `_parse_llm_json` at `extract.py` L3481–3485: replaces strict `if not text.startswith("["): return []` with a preamble-tolerant cascade (`text = raw.lstrip(); bracket_idx = text.find("["); if bracket_idx == -1: return []; text = text[bracket_idx:]`). Handles the common failure mode where the LLM wraps its truncated JSON array in a preamble like `"Here is the extracted JSON array:\n\`\`\`\n[\n  {..."`. Byte-identical behavior when `raw` starts with `[` or contains no `[` at all; the brace-balanced salvage walker (`_salvage_truncated_array`) does the rest, recovering any complete `{...}` objects from the prefix regardless of wrapping. `probe_02` (same 5-case pack) confirmed the patch works: **0/5 → 2/5 pass, avg score 0.140 → 0.620**; 4 of 5 silent events flipped `parse_drop` → successful parse (the 5th was already producing items via fallback). New `validator_drop` signal revealed on case_077 `great_grandparents.*` / case_081 `grandparents.ancestry` — maps perfectly to pending task **#119 turnscope `allowed_branches` fix for greatGrandparents subtree**, which now has fresh evidence. Next tag = **r5f** (master with Phase 1 + Phase 2 composite, running at time of this writeup). Prediction (Chris, three-agent convergence): 61–63/104, v2 33–35, mnw 0–2 (no worse than r5e1 floor), with silent-output class dropping sharply and being replaced by validator/turnscope failures on #119's target class. Methodology flag: r5f is a 2-commit stack (Phase 1 instrumentation-only + Phase 2 one-line parser tolerance) landed on top of r5e1 baseline; any flips attributable to Phase 1 are impossible by construction (zero extractor-behavior change), so any flip is traceable to the parser-tolerance patch. Salvage patch is byte-stable on the "clean JSON" path, which means r5e1's 59 passing cases should remain at least 59; flips downward would indicate an unexpected interaction worth rollback — flips upward are the expected outcome and should be audited per the standard post-eval block before adoption. Root cause of the 6 HTTP 500s on extract-fields: SPANTAG Pass 2 LLM occasionally returns `value` as bool (`True`), int (`2024`), or list (`['Gretchen', 'Amelia', 'Cole']`) instead of a string, which fails Pydantic `value: str` validation and bubbles up as ASGI 500. Patch defensively coerces (`bool→"yes"/"no"`, `int/float→str(...)`, `list→", ".join(...)`, `dict→json.dumps(...)`, `None→drop`), logs each coercion via new `[extract][value-coerce]` log marker (so BINDING-01 can survey production occurrences), and wraps the construction in try/except so a single bad item drops the item rather than killing the whole response. The principled fix (split list values into N items, surface bool sentinels via the no_writes channel, etc.) belongs in BINDING-01; this is containment. (2) `server/code/api/api.py:50` — defensive HF cache offline mode. Sets `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1` + `TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1` at module init when MODEL_PATH contains slashes (i.e., is a local directory). Fixes `huggingface_hub.errors.HFValidationError: Repo id must be in the form 'repo_name' or 'namespace/repo_name': '/mnt/c/models/hornelore/Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct'` raised by `/api/warmup` when `_load_model()` re-call invokes `try_to_load_from_cache(repo_id)` with the local path. `local_files_only=True` on `from_pretrained` is not enough on its own — the cache lookup runs first. Defensive; primarily fixes the warmup endpoint. (3) `.env` — `HORNELORE_SPANTAG=1 → 0`, with explanatory comment. SPANTAG cap env vars (`SPANTAG_PASS1_MAX_NEW=1024`, `SPANTAG_PASS2_MAX_NEW=1536`) stay in `.env` so they're available the moment SPANTAG re-enables under BINDING-01. **Active sequence reorder:** #152 BINDING-01 promoted to TOP of queue (was parallel cleanup lane); SPANTAG demoted from active extractor lane to "evidence pipeline" — SPANTAG default-on remains gated until BINDING-01 lands null-safety + binding hallucination containment. #97 VALUE-ALT-CREDIT and #144 SCHEMA-ANCESTOR-EXPAND drop one slot. **Active baseline unchanged: r5h (70/104, v3=41/62, v2=35/62, mnw=2).** Files changed: `server/code/api/routers/extract.py` (value-coerce patch at L7006), `server/code/api/api.py` (HF offline init at L50), `.env` (SPANTAG=0 + comment). - 2026-04-30 (full day, WO-LORI-STORY-CAPTURE-01 Phase 1A + 1B end-to-end): **Story preservation lane went from spec to live row in production today.** Banked 11 commits across two sequenced WO landings. The big moment: candidate `9bb91643-e2aa-49f6-bd38-b57e3a45c3e0` is the first preserved story_candidate row in the live DB, captured from a Lori session where Janice gave the canonical mastoidectomy text — proving the entire Path 1 pipeline (trigger → preserve → DB) fires correctly behind the `HORNELORE_STORY_CAPTURE=1` flag. **Spec landings (early day):** `WO-LORI-STORY-CAPTURE-01_Spec.md` authored with golfball architecture (CORE/WINDINGS/COVER/PARTNER), 6 LAWs with 4-classification system (RUBBERBAND behavioral / WALL deterministic / STRUCTURAL system flow / INFRASTRUCTURE impossible-to-violate-in-code), Path 1 (preservation, must succeed) decoupled from Path 2 (extraction, best-effort). New `docs/golfball/` lineage folder with README + 2026-04-30 dialogue capturing the architectural origin. **Phase 1A Commits (mid-day):** (1) **Commit 1 d85d517 — schema + db.py stubs + LAW 3 isolation test:** new `0004_story_candidates.sql` migration (id UUID PK, narrator_id, transcript, audio_clip_path, audio_duration_sec, word_count, trigger_reason enum, scene_anchor_count, era_candidates JSON, age_bucket, estimated_year_low/high, confidence, scene_anchors JSON, extraction_status, extracted_fields JSON, review_status, review_notes, reviewed_at, reviewed_by, session_id, conversation_id, turn_id, created_at — 3 indexes on narrator_id + review_status + created_at DESC); 6 db.py accessor stubs raising RuntimeError; new `tests/test_story_preservation_isolation.py` AST-walks story_preservation.py imports transitively, FAILS BUILD if any forbidden prefix (api.routers.extract / api.prompt_composer / api.memory_echo / api.routers.llm_* / api.routers.chat_ws / api.routers.family_truth) appears. Found-and-fixed bug in the AST collector itself during negative verification (was missing `from X import Y` → `X.Y` form). (2) **Commit 2 9cb750f — preservation bodies + age arithmetic + 56 unit tests:** filled the db.py stubs with real sqlite implementations + enum validation; new `server/code/api/services/story_preservation.py` with `preserve_turn` / `update_placement` / `get_unreviewed` / `get_candidate`; new `server/code/api/services/age_arithmetic.py` with DOB+bucket→year arithmetic, multi-era ambiguity preserved as list ("before_school" → ["earliest_years", "early_school_years"]) — bucket→age table covers very_little/before_school/preschool/early_school/in_school/grade_school/older/adolescent/teenager/young_adult/coming_of_age. (3) **Commit 3a 5b6aa7d — story trigger module + env gates:** new `server/code/api/services/story_trigger.py` pure functions, two trigger paths (full_threshold = duration≥30s AND words≥60 AND ≥1 anchor; borderline_scene_anchor = ≥3 anchors). Anchor detection across 3 dimensions: PLACE / RELATIVE_TIME / PERSON_RELATION, each contributes ≤1 to count. Conservative bigram detection — bare "remember", bare "Dad", yes/no answers don't fire. Env-tunable thresholds (`STORY_TRIGGER_MIN_DURATION_SEC=30`, `STORY_TRIGGER_MIN_WORDS=60`, `STORY_TRIGGER_BORDERLINE_ANCHOR_COUNT=3`). `HORNELORE_STORY_CAPTURE=0` master switch added to `.env.example`. (4) **Bare-noun place fix 205d30f:** reviewer caught implementation/docstring divergence — "School was hard" / "Kitchen was tidy" were firing place anchors against stated intent because `_PLACE_NOUNS` was substring-matched. Split into Tier 1 (BARE: hospital/church/factory/plant/mill/fairgrounds — fire on bare mention) and Tier 2 (PREP_REQUIRED: school/kitchen/yard/library/etc. — only fire after place preposition). Word-boundary regex on both tiers (kills the `implant`-matches-`plant` substring bug). +11 lock-in tests. (5) **plant/mill demotion b75a9ba:** reviewer round 2 caught "The plant died" / "They mill around" still overfiring — both nouns are also common nouns/verbs. Demoted plant + mill from Tier 1 to Tier 2. Widened the prep regex's adjective slot to allow optional determiner + 0–2 generic lowercase tokens before the noun (so "at the aluminum plant" still fires while "at home with my plant" does not). +7 lock-in tests. Janice canonical still hits 3 anchors. (6) **Commit 3b ac4c56d — chat_ws wiring + idempotency:** the load-bearing one. Single insertion at `chat_ws.py` L198 between person_id extract and the archive_ensure_session block. Lazy gated imports (`HORNELORE_STORY_CAPTURE=0` keeps preservation modules out of `sys.modules` entirely — runtime LAW 3 INFRASTRUCTURE assertion). Empty/whitespace transcript skipped silently. `trigger_diagnostic()` runs every non-empty turn → `[story-trigger]` log marker carries trigger / words / anchors / place / time / person. `preserve_turn()` only fires when `trigger != None AND person_id present`. Missing-narrator emits `[story-trigger] WARNING` (not silent drop). preserve_turn failure → `[CRITICAL] preserve_turn FAILED ... session continues but story was NOT saved` with `exc_info=True`, NO rethrow — chat turn always continues per LAW 3. **Idempotency:** `turn_id` threads through chat_ws → preserve_turn → db row; new accessor `db.story_candidate_get_by_turn(narrator_id, turn_id)` follows existing house style (`_connect` / `try-finally` / `_row_to_story_candidate`); preserve_turn checks `(narrator_id, turn_id)` at top, returns existing id with `[preserve] dedupe-skip` log marker if found, else fresh insert. turn_id opt-in (no key → no dedupe by design). +4 dedupe tests + 3 accessor tests = 111/111 pass. **Eval landing same day:** `r5h-place-guard-v2` master eval = 75/110 v3=46/68 v2=40/68 mnw=2 (5 of 6 new BUG-EX-PLACE-LASTNAME-01 fixtures case_105–110 pass; case_110 fails on `field_path_mismatch` `family.spouse.*` vs `spouse.*` — separate lane, place-guard ITSELF fires correctly). Underlying 104-case score unchanged at 70/104. **Phase 1A polish (early evening):** 7-patch hardening commit covering the live-runtime bug sweep — (A) SYSTEM_QF directive skip in chat_ws gate (UI sends `[SYSTEM_QF: ...]` as user-role messages; without skip those would write false-positive rows the moment a directive happened to mention 3 anchors), (B) schema-drift sanity check in init_db (PRAGMA table_info compared against `_CRITICAL_SCHEMA` map, logs ERROR on drift, non-fatal — catches hand-edited table mismatches that would otherwise produce cryptic "no such column" errors at INSERT time), (C) transcript soft cap at MAX_TRANSCRIPT_BYTES=50_000 with `" [...truncated]"` sentinel (defensive against runaway STT), (D) dedupe path null-id guard (falls through to fresh insert + WARNING if existing_id is falsy), (E) turn_id whitespace normalization (`(params.get("turn_id") or "").strip() or None`), (F) scene_anchors round-trip already covered by test_preserve_minimal, (G) `datetime.utcnow()` → `datetime.now(timezone.utc)` deprecation cleanup. +3 new tests for C + D = 114/114 pass. Optional narrator-id filter input + manual Refresh button. Auto-loads on DOMContentLoaded + window focus. Phase 1B is intentionally read-only — promote/refine/discard land in Phase 3. **Real-world friction observed in live test, not yet fixed:** the trigger classifier is too tight for narrator-shaped text — lowercase "spokane" doesn't match the proper-noun regex (requires capital `[A-Z]`); "because of the war" doesn't match the relative-time patterns (only `during/in the war` fires); bare "he" doesn't fire person anchor (only `my dad/mom/etc.`). The Janice-canonical capitalized text fires 3 anchors and lands a row; real narrator turns from STT or quick typing tend to land at 1–2 anchors and miss borderline. Two paths forward sized for the next session: lower `STORY_TRIGGER_BORDERLINE_ANCHOR_COUNT` from 3 to 2 via env (no code change, immediate), OR tighten classifier for case-insensitive proper nouns + add "because of the war" / "after the war" patterns + consider bare "Dad/Mom" with capital-only matching (~15 min code change + tests). **Active tasks updated:** #341 Phase 1A Commit 1, #342 Commit 2, #343 Commit 3a all completed; new completed work for Commit 3b + polish + Phase 1B + bug sweep doc + first-row landing. Architectural rules baked in: relational subject patterns checked BEFORE bare self pronoun ("My dad" → parent, not self+possessive); negated clauses suppress all field hints; uncertain clauses still emit hints (extractor decides downgrade); place-prep regex accepts case-insensitive prepositions but capitalized place name ONLY (intentionally rejects re.IGNORECASE on `[A-Z]` to avoid the "Stanley too" / "be alive anymore" false-positive that surfaced in early sanity test); confidence scoring (high/partial/low) gates downstream consumer behavior. **20 hand-authored fixture utterances** at `tests/fixtures/utterance_frame_cases.json` with partial-shape assertion JSON (NOT byte-equality — frame is allowed to fill more slots than the test asserts; it must NOT contradict an assertion). - 2026-05-05 (morning + afternoon, BUG-LORI-REFLECTION-02 Patch C + SAFETY-INTEGRATION-01 Phase 2 + harness lori_reply capture fix + 4 BUG specs banked + TEST-23 v6 + Phase 1c era-stories spec): **Eight commits across two main landings + four BUG specs + harness rename + Phase 1c spec authored.** (1) **BUG-LORI-REFLECTION-02 Patch C runtime shaping LANDED end-to-end** behind `HORNELORE_REFLECTION_SHAPING=0` default-off flag — locked Patch B principle ("prompt-heavy reflection makes Lori worse, runtime shaping in code is the answer") implemented as three layers in `server/code/api/services/lori_reflection.py`: Layer 1 `extract_concrete_anchor()` selects ONE anchor from narrator text using priority cascade — multi-word proper noun phrases (capitalized mid-sentence + sentence-start with verb-lookahead pattern) > kinship anchors ("My dad" → "Your father", "my mom" → "Your mother") > content tokens (≥4 informative non-stopword tokens), with `_PROPER_NOUN_BLOCKLIST` (I/We/The/Monday/Today/etc) preventing pronoun + day-name false positives; Layer 2 `shape_reflection()` Cases A/B/C1/C2/D — Case A trivial narrator text passes Lori response through unchanged; Case B no-echo + anchor present → prepend "Your father — " or "Stanley — " to first sentence; Case C1 echo too long with anchor inside → trim echo to anchor + comma; Case C2 echo too long no anchor → drop echo entirely keeping the substantive reply; Case D pass through; Layer 3 softened-mode 30-word first-sentence cap runs FIRST before any other case detection so distress turns get small responses regardless of anchor selection. Constants `SHAPER_ECHO_WORD_BUDGET=25`, `SHAPER_SOFTENED_TURN_BUDGET=30`, `_TRIVIAL_CONTENT_TOKEN_THRESHOLD=4`. Wired into `lori_communication_control.py` Step 3.5 (between word-limit + validate_memory_echo) for main path AND `_safety_path()` for softened-mode with separate try/except. Verification log marker `[life-map][era-click] Lori prompt dispatched for era=<canonical>`. **Lane A — old spoken silence-cue gated** — single chokepoint at top of `lv80FireCheckIn()` in `ui/hornelore1.0.html` (~line 6088). - 2026-05-07 (evening night-shift, multilingual phase 5 + composer locale pack — **8 commits banked: 5C-5F + 2 bug fixes + composer Spanish + parser overcapture fix + README + 4 bug specs + CLAUDE.md changelog**): After the morning Melanie Zollner work landed, ran a focused autonomous evening on multilingual surfaces. **Phase 5C verified** (`ui/js/app.js` Spanish name-extraction triggers — `me llamo` / `mi nombre es` / `yo soy` / etc., plus accent-preserving `_NAME_CAP` regex `[A-ZÁÉÍÓÚÑ][A-Za-zÁÉÍÓÚÑáéíóúñ'-]+...{0,3}` — 28-case node functional probe green; capture preserves "María Núñez" / "José Luis" intact; Spanish stopwords `yo/soy/sí/pues/vale/hola` added to `_NOT_A_NAME` blocklist; `_EMOTIONAL_MARKERS_ES` regex catches `difícil/triste/dolor/muerte` to prevent distress turns being parsed as names). **Phase 5D landed** (Spanish phantom-noun whitelist in `services/lori_communication_control.py`): kinship (`Mamá`, `Abuela`, `Tía`, `Hijo`), calendar (`Lunes`, `Enero`, `Verano`), religious (`Dios`, `Cristo`, `Virgen`), holiday (`Navidad`, `Pascua`), Q-words (`Qué`, `Cuándo`, `Dónde`, `Cómo`) added to `_PHANTOM_NOUN_WHITELIST` plus mid-sentence proper-noun detection regex extended to accept Spanish accent capitals `[Á É Í Ó Ú Ñ]` so `María` / `Núñez` get detected as candidates the same way English ones are; real personal names + country names (`María` `José` `Núñez` `México` `España` `Cuba` `Perú`) deliberately NOT whitelisted because narrator-corpus + profile-seed verification gate is the correct mechanism for those. 15 new tests including regression checks. **Phase 5E landed** (`compose_correction_ack` Spanish branch): detects narrator language via `looks_spanish()` at composer entry; emits Spanish acknowledgments (`Lo entiendo — dos hijos, no el número que tenía. Disculpa la confusión.`); number words emit Spanish (`uno, dos, tres, ...`); field labels translate (`tu padre era X` / `tu madre era X` / `naciste en X`). Extended `looks_spanish()` `_SPANISH_FUNCTION_WORDS` list with high-confidence Spanish-only verb conjugations (`tengo, tienes, tenemos, tienen, dijo, vine, hice, quiso, vivía, hablaba, decía`), infinitives (`decir, hablar, vivir, escribir, querer, poder, tener, venir`), and Spanish-only number words 2-15 + 20/30/100/1000 (`dos, tres, cuatro, ..., quince, veinte, treinta, cien, mil`) so unaccented Spanish text like "no, tengo dos hijos, no tres" gets detected. 9 new ack tests. **Phase 5F landed** (12-case code-switching eval pack at `data/evals/lori_code_switching_eval.json`): EN→ES intro / ES→EN emotional shift / ES correction after EN Lori / pure-Spanish birthplace + father-name + meant-pattern corrections / ES grandmother-narrative (BUG-ML-SHADOW-EXTRACT-PLACE-AS-BIRTHPLACE-01 evidence case) / code-switch with kinship-term gloss / phantom-noun avoidance / short Spanish "sí" confirmation / mid-sentence language flip / dangling demonstrative question close / full Spanish multi-detail intro. 14 fixture-shape tests + functional probes via parser/looks_spanish. **BUG-ML-LORI-SPANISH-FRAGMENT-REPAIR-02 landed** (`services/lori_spanish_guard.py`): added demonstrative determiners (`esas, esos, esa, ese, estas, estos, esta, este, aquellas, aquellos, aquella, aquel`) and additional possessives (`mi, mis, tu, tus, nuestra, nuestro, nuestras, nuestros`) to `_FRAGMENT_CONNECTORS`; new `_has_unclosed_spanish_question(text)` helper counts `¿` vs `?` openers; the fragment guard closes a trimmed fragment with `?` when there's an unclosed `¿` upstream, otherwise `.`. Live failure: *"¿Qué recuerdas de cómo sonaba su voz cuando contaba esas."* now repairs to *"¿Qué recuerdas de cómo sonaba su voz cuando contaba?"*. 9 new fragment tests; 57 total guard tests green. **BUG-ML-SHADOW-EXTRACT-PLACE-AS-BIRTHPLACE-01 landed** (`server/code/api/routers/extract.py`): post-LLM regex guard mirroring BUG-EX-PLACE-LASTNAME-01 architecture. Drops `*.birthPlace` / `*.placeOfBirth` candidates whose value appears in source text only after narrative-connection verbs (English: `talked about / missed / remembered / stories of`; Spanish: `hablaba de / extrañaba / recordaba / contaba historias de`) and never with explicit birth-evidence (English: `was born in/at/near`; Spanish: `nació en, era de, originario de, vino al mundo en`). Whisper accent-strip variants included. Sibling lane to PLACE-LASTNAME-01; runs independently (no SPANTAG flag). 26 new tests. Live failure (Melanie Carter Spanish session): `grandparents.birthPlace=Peru` from `"Cuando mi abuela hablaba de Perú, decía que extrañaba las montañas..."` now correctly dropped — narrator never said the grandmother was *born* in Perú, only that she *talked about* it. **README "Multilingual narrator support" section added** documenting all 12 wired EN/ES surfaces (STT auto-detect / LANGUAGE MIRRORING / perspective + fragment guards / question-quality detector / phantom-noun guard / name extraction / correction parser+ack / place-as-birthplace guard / story trigger anchors / era warm labels / memoir export / pluggable TTS) + the 4 not-yet-wired surfaces (Kokoro TTS runtime patches, Spanish-monolingual eval suite, deterministic-composer Spanish branches, fully-wired live verify). **6-commit night-shift bank** + **commit 7 (Phase 5C app.js) appended** (the morning's Spanish name-extraction triggers — required separate commit since I'd assumed it was already on disk during the bank): commits e7d42ad / ab8be62 / 791b7a9 / 9b0bd31 / e0dd746 / 715bd67 / `[Phase 5C app.js to-commit]` landed cleanly + Chris pushed. **Continuing post-commit work tonight (A → B → C plan):** **(A) BUG-ML-LORI-DETERMINISTIC-COMPOSERS-ENGLISH-ONLY-01 Phase 1 LANDED** (`compose_memory_echo` Spanish locale pack): added `_MEMORY_ECHO_LOCALE` module-level dict with 30 keys × 2 locales (`en` + `es`), all narrator-facing strings now resolve from the pack (`Esto es lo que sé de {name} hasta ahora:` / `Identidad` / `Familia` / `(aún sin registrar)` / `Lo que aún no tengo claro` / etc.); new `_translate_relation()` helper translates projection-relation labels (`Father → Padre`, `Mother → Madre`, `Brother → Hermano`, `Sister → Hermana`, plus 20 other kinship terms); `compose_memory_echo` accepts `target_language="en"` (default) keyword param; `_fmt_line_explicit` accepts `target_language` for placeholder localization; chat_ws.py call site detects narrator language via `looks_spanish(user_text)` and threads `target_language="es"` when triggered (try/except non-fatal — defaults to "en" on any error); promoted-truth section translates field labels via `_PROMOTED_FIELD_LABEL_KEYS` map + uses Spanish "X de Y" possessive form ("ocupación de María: doctor") instead of English "Y's X"; era-warm-label call passes `target_language` through `era_id_to_warm_label(era_id, target_language)` (function already accepts locale arg from earlier WO-ML-03A); source-footer labels (`profile → perfil`, `interview projection → proyección de entrevista`, etc.) emit in narrator's language; defensive fallback to "en" when target_language is unknown. 32 new tests covering locale-pack shape parity (en/es key count match), translate_relation (7 cases), English-default byte-stability regression (2 cases), Spanish header / missing fields / family rendering / profile_seed / uncertain section / footer / no-records / promoted facts (named + unnamed + other-subject) / unknown-locale fallback. All 32 pass; 153 prior multilingual tests still green. **(B) BUG-ML-LORI-CORRECTION-PARSER-VALUE-OVERCAPTURE-01 LANDED** (`server/code/api/memory_echo.py`): the live parser bug where `"no, nací en Lima, no en Cuzco"` captured `"Lima, no en Cuzco"` as the full birthplace value instead of stopping at the retraction clause. Eight value-capture patterns affected (4 EN: birthplace, father-was, mother-was; 4 ES: nacio-en, padre-llamaba, padre-era, madre-llamaba, madre-era — wait, 5 ES + 3 EN = 8). Fix: non-greedy capture `+?` + optional retraction tail group `(?:,\s+no(?:\s+en)?\s+([A-Za-zÁÉÍÓÚÑáéíóúñ .'\-]+?))?` (Spanish) / `(?:,\s+not\s+([A-Za-z .'\-]+?))?` (English) at end of regex; new `_capture_retraction()` helper appends retraction value to `out['_retracted']`. Comma KEPT in value char class so legitimate `"Buenos Aires, Argentina"` / `"Lima, Perú"` still parse correctly (verified via regression tests). 14 new tests in new `ValueOvercaptureFixTest` class: ES birthplace+father+mother with retraction, EN birthplace+father+mother with retraction, multipart-place preserved, simple cases byte-stable. The Phase 5F fixture test `test_cs_004_birthplace_correction_parses` workaround `startswith("Lima")` upgraded to strict `assertEqual("Lima")` + retraction assertion since parser now produces correct shape. 88 total parser tests green. **(C) CLAUDE.md changelog entry — this entry**. **Files changed (post-commit-7):** `server/code/api/prompt_composer.py` (+~180 lines: locale pack + helpers + branch logic in compose_memory_echo + _fmt_line_explicit signature change), `server/code/api/memory_echo.py` (+~40 lines: 8 regex patterns refactored + retraction helper), `server/code/api/routers/chat_ws.py` (+~12 lines: looks_spanish detection at memory_echo call site), `tests/test_compose_memory_echo_spanish.py` (NEW, +32 tests), `tests/test_memory_echo_correction_parser.py` (+~110 lines, +14 ValueOvercaptureFixTest class), `tests/test_code_switching_eval_fixtures.py` (cs_004 strict assertion restored). **Active baseline unchanged:** `r5h-followup-guard-v1` (78/114, v3=49/72, v2=43/72, mnw=2). SPANTAG=0 default. **Live verification queue after stack restart:** (1) Spanish narrator memory_echo turn → confirm fully-Spanish readback (no English code-switch); (2) Fragment-repair-02 — `?` close on dangling demonstrative; (3) Place-as-birthplace guard — `hablaba de Perú` does NOT route to grandparents.birthPlace; (4) Phantom-noun guard — Spanish kinship/calendar tokens NOT flagged; (5) Phase 5E correction-ack — Spanish narrator's "no, tengo dos hijos" gets `Lo entiendo —` ack; (6) Parser overcapture-fix — `"nací en Lima, no en Cuzco"` → place=Lima + _retracted=[Cuzco]. **Remaining gaps for future iteration:** `compose_age_recall` + `compose_continuation_paraphrase` still English-only (Phase 2 + 3 of BUG-ML-LORI-DETERMINISTIC-COMPOSERS-ENGLISH-ONLY-01); Kokoro TTS engine swap pending live verify of perspective fix; Spanish-monolingual eval suite parallel to 114-case master pack still open. Cuando mi abuela hablaba de Perú, yo escuchaba su voz y me sentía cerca de ella."* — 21 words, ~15s, place + person anchors = 2 anchors; was failing all 3 prior gates. New gate: PLACE + (PERSON or TIME) AND words ≥ `STORY_TRIGGER_RICH_SHORT_MIN_WORDS` (default 15) AND duration ≥ `STORY_TRIGGER_RICH_SHORT_MIN_DURATION_SEC` (default 10s). - 2026-05-09 (Mary's session triage — **5 parent-session blocker fixes LANDED** end-to-end + 5 BUG specs banked): Mary's session at 12:45-14:05 surfaced a stack of failures: (1) **988 false-positive** on "I am kind of scared, are you safe to talk to?" — Lori dispatched a suicide hotline to an 86yo who was anxious about the AI itself; (2) **identity denial** — "what is your name" → "I don't have a name, I'm just a listener and a guide" (flat contradiction of `prompt_composer.py:34`); (3) **question ignore** — Mary's third "what is your name again and your purpose" pivoted to a continuation-walk template; (4) **"AI." 3-char stub** — "what is an AI?" twice produced byte-identical 3-char "AI." responses; (5) **extractor fragment write** — `personal.fullName = "scared about talking to"` from Mary's panic phrase; (6) **era confabulation** — line 62 "you mentioned you had kids and moved around for your husband's work" during Mary's Early School Years (ages 6-12), and line 119 "you were also wondering earlier about the current date" when Mary never asked; both violations of CLAUDE.md design principle 7 ("Mechanical truth must visibly project"). **Five fixes landed:** **(A) BUG-LORI-IDENTITY-META-QUESTION-DETERMINISTIC-ROUTE-01** — new pure-stdlib `services/lori_meta_question.py` module (370 lines, LAW 3 isolated). 5 categories: identity_name, identity_what, purpose, safety_concern, capability_explain. EN+ES locale pack with Lorevox etymology in identity answers ("Lore means stories and oral tradition; Vox is Latin for voice. Together: the voice of your stories"). Priority ordering: safety > capability > what > name+purpose > name. Wired into `chat_ws.py` BEFORE the safety scan (line 605 area, immediately after `_safety_result = None`); when matched, skips safety scan entirely AND overrides `turn_mode = "meta_question"`. New dispatcher branch emits the deterministic answer via WS, persists via existing `persist_turn_transaction` path. Default-on, no env flag (correctness fix not feature). 58 unit tests + 4 LAW 3 isolation gate tests; Mary's literal 5 turns appear as named tests so any regression on her exact phrasing fails the build. **(B) BUG-LORI-SAFETY-FALSE-POSITIVE-EXTERNAL-FEAR-01** — defense-in-depth on the LLM second-layer classifier in case the regex misses a future class. New env-tunable `HORNELORE_SAFETY_LLM_CONFIDENCE_FLOOR=0.65` floor on `should_route_to_safety()` for distressed/ideation only (acute always routes regardless of confidence — explicit self-harm must never be filtered). Pattern-side detections in `safety.py` are unaffected (they have their own per-pattern thresholds). `_SYSTEM_PROMPT` extended with CRITICAL DISTINCTION block teaching the LLM that "scared/afraid/anxious OF or ABOUT [external thing]" is `none`, with Mary's literal phrasing as a "must classify as none" example. 12 new tests (`SafetyFalsePositiveExternalFearTest` + `SafetyPromptExternalFearGuidanceTest`); existing `test_llm_distressed_ideation_acute_route_when_pattern_none` updated to assert distressed-at-0.5 does NOT route (Mary's class) and acute-at-0.30 still does. Pre-existing 11-case `IndirectIdeationRedTeamMiniPack` unchanged (mock confidence 0.75 above floor). **(C) BUG-LORI-RESPONSE-STUB-COLLAPSE-01** — operator-visibility detection for any stub-collapse that escapes the meta-question intercept. New Step 6 in `lori_communication_control.py`: when final response is ≤3 words AND narrator's input was substantive (≥4 words AND not safety-triggered), append `response_stub_collapse` to failures. Detection-only in v1 — replacing real short answers with fabricated content would backfire on legitimate brevity ("Yes." in response to a yes-no is fine). 5 new tests in `StubCollapseDetectionTest` covering Mary's "AI." literal, single-word "Spokane.", normal multi-word response, trivial yes/no narrator, 3-word narrator boundary. **(D) BUG-EX-PROTECTED-IDENTITY-FRAGMENT-WRITE-01** — sibling to BUG-EX-PLACE-LASTNAME-01 + BUG-ML-SHADOW-EXTRACT-PLACE-AS-BIRTHPLACE-01 architecture. New helpers in `extract.py`: `_AFFECT_DISTRESS_TOKENS` (EN+ES affect vocabulary including Whisper accent-strip variants), `_AFFECT_DISTRESS_PHRASES` (regex prefixes "I am scared" / "estoy asustada" / "tengo miedo" / etc.), `_AFFECT_GUARDED_FIELD_SUFFIXES` (.fullName / .firstName / .lastName / .middleName / .maidenName / .preferredName), `_value_starts_with_affect_token(value)`, `_looks_like_affect_phrase_for_value(text, value)`, `_drop_affect_phrase_as_name(item, source_text)`. Two independent triggers — EITHER drops the candidate: (1) value starts with affect/distress vocabulary (Mary's literal failure mode: value="scared about talking to"); (2) value appears in source after distress phrase ("I'm afraid of [name]"). Wired into the per-item loop in `extract_fields()` right after the existing two PLACE guards. Pure post-LLM cleanup, no SPANTAG flag, ships independently. 32 new tests across 6 classes (`MaryLiteralFailureMode` + `ValueStartsWithDistressTokenTest` + `SpanishDistressPrefixTest` + `IndirectDistressPhraseTest` + `FalsePositiveResistanceTest` + `HelperFunctionTest`); stubs FastAPI + pydantic in test setup mirroring `test_extract_place_birthplace_guard.py` pattern. Master eval impact: zero on locked baseline `r5h-followup-guard-v1` (78/114) since none of the 114 cases exercise this exact pattern. **(E) BUG-LORI-ERA-CONFABULATION-01** — directive-only fix in `prompt_composer.py`.
